Under sub-Weibull noise, we establish selection consistency through approximation-error separation without relying on RIP-type conditions, together with nonasymptotic oracle risk bounds that remain valid under model misspecification. The same coding principle places heterogeneous classes on a common complexity scale at a small additional class-identification cost. This extension yields class--model recovery under suitable identifiability conditions and risk adaptation across classes. We further develop a complexity-guided search path that makes the computation--statistics trade-off explicit. Large penalties yield polynomial-size retained search regions with high probability, whereas smaller penalties sharpen the oracle risk benchmark. Numerical experiments illustrate stable support recovery and favorable estimation performance under strong dependence and model-class uncertainty.

</details>


### [227] [Incremental Recommendation via Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26804)
*Athanasios Vlontzos,David Gustafsson,Michael O'Riordan,Ciarán M. Gilligan-Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双阈值因果推荐策略，利用已有的holdback数据（无需新数据采集）将生产推荐模型扩展为因果架构，解决治疗组与对照组归因窗口不匹配问题，在Spotify千万级用户A/B测试中将推荐展示量降低7%且推荐内容消费无显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐展示是稀缺资源，如果用户本来就会自然发现内容，则推送该推荐不会产生增量价值，反而挤占了其他更有价值的推荐机会。因此需要识别并避免这种"非增量"的推荐，以提高推荐系统的效率。

Method: 将已有的生产推荐模型扩展为因果架构，利用常规实验基础设施中已收集的holdback数据（无需额外数据采集）。针对治疗组（短直接响应窗口归因）与对照组（多日有机流归因）归因窗口不匹配的问题，设计双阈值目标选择策略：当治疗流概率高且有机流概率低时才推送推荐。同时采用联合训练方式融合holdback数据。

Result: 在Spotify数百万用户的规模化A/B测试中，该策略将推荐展示量降低7%，且推荐内容整体消费无统计显著下降。联合训练方法相比生产基线改善了治疗头（treated head）的校准效果，表明因果模型比仅基于观测数据训练的模型学到更泛化的表示。

Conclusion: 因果推荐模型结合holdback数据可以有效削减非增量推荐，节省稀缺的推荐展示资源而不损失总体内容消费；联合训练带来的校准改进可作为因果模型学得更泛化表示的佐证。

Abstract: Recommendation impressions are a finite resource, hence delivering a recommendation to a user who would discover the content organically yields no incremental value and displaces other recommendations that could. We address this by extending an existing production recommendation model to a causal architecture using holdback data that is already collected as part of routine experimentation infrastructure, requiring no new data collection. A central challenge is that attribution windows differ between treated and holdback observations: treated users are attributed a stream within a short direct-response window, while holdback users are attributed organic streams over a multi-day window. This mismatch makes naive treatment-effect subtraction invalid. We resolve this with a dual-threshold targeting policy that delivers a recommendation only when the probability of a treated stream is high and the probability of organic stream is low. In a production-scale A/B test on millions of Spotify users, this policy reduces recommendation impressions by 7% with no statistically significant reduction in overall recommended content consumption. We further show that joint training with holdback data improves calibration of the treated head relative to the production baseline, and argue this can be taken as evidence that causal models learn more generalisable representations than models trained on observational data alone.

</details>


### [228] [Why not to use the Gaussian kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26974)
*Toni Karvonen,Chris J. Oates*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文论证高斯核（亦称平方指数核、径向基函数核）不应作为默认选择，因为它极脆弱：一方面导致条件方差过小而引发预测过度自信，另一方面伴随数值病态问题需借助nugget等技巧修正。作者进一步指出问题根源在于核的解析性（等价于谱密度指数衰减），因此任何解析核都应避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核是高斯过程回归中最常用的核函数之一，但作者认为其存在系统性缺陷，会导致预测不确定性被严重低估以及数值计算病态。该文旨在提醒研究者不应将高斯核作为默认选择，并将问题推广到更广泛的解析核类别。

Method: 作者通过两个理论结果论证高斯核的脆弱性：其一，高斯核产生不切实际地过小的条件方差，若用于量化预测不确定性则几乎必然导致灾难性过度自信；其二，小方差伴随数值病态问题，实践中需借助nugget项等技巧，这实质上改变了底层回归或分类模型。作者将根本原因归结为高斯核的解析性（对平稳核而言等同于谱密度的指数衰减），从而推广到一般解析核的批判。

Result: 两个关键结果：(1) 高斯核导致条件方差过小，使不确定性量化产生过度自信；(2) 高斯核引发数值病态条件，必须采用nugget等技巧修正。问题的根源是核的解析性即谱密度的指数衰减。

Conclusion: 高斯核这类解析核应当被避免使用，不应作为默认选择。作者建议使用非解析的核函数，例如马特恩核，以获得更稳健的不确定性估计和数值行为。

Abstract: Kernels measure similarity or correlation in tasks such as regression and classification. The Gaussian kernel, other names of which include squared exponential and radial basis function kernel, is one of the most popular in Gaussian process regression. We argue that the Gaussian kernel is best avoided and should never be used as a default. The argument rests on two results demonstrating that the Gaussian kernel is extremely brittle. First, the Gaussian kernel gives rise to a conditional variance that is unrealistically small. If the variance is used to quantify predictive uncertainty, catastrophic overconfidence is almost inevitable. Second, a small variance goes hand in hand with numerical ill-conditioning, so that to use the Gaussian kernel in practice requires tricks such as nugget terms that effectively modify the underlying regression or classification model. These problems are caused by the unnatural smoothness of the Gaussian kernel, a fact we are far from the first to take notice of. The problem is not the Gaussian form itself but the analyticity of the kernel: Our argument is more broadly that analytic kernels are best avoided. For stationary kernels analyticity is essentially equivalent to an exponential decay of the spectral density.

</details>


### [229] [Representation Measurements Under Function-Preserving Reparameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27020)
*Abdullah Karasan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明列置换平行分析（column-permutation parallel analysis）不满足函数保持重参数化不变性，导致其成分数量估计与决策受隐藏坐标系选择影响，而非反映模型本身的固有属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型隐藏坐标并非由输入输出函数唯一确定，因此从表示衍生出的测量指标应对保持函数不变的基变换具有不变性。作者旨在检验平行分析这类基于排列的表示测量方法是否违反这一原则。

Method: 通过理论分析证明列置换平行分析违反函数保持重参数化不变性；通过实验在5个模型、3个检索领域、75种变换下比较平行分析的成分计数与决策，并设置仅中心化对照组与独立平行分析种子对照组来隔离参考分布效应；同时对比正交不变比较器分数的稳定性。

Result: 成分计数中位差异为0.79，固定阈值决策中位差异为0.26；中心化对照组中1200个成分计数有1141个发生变化而观测谱不变，独立平行分析种子未改变任何相应决策；正交不变比较器分数保持数值稳定且保留类似的留出判别性能。

Conclusion: 平行分析推导出的成分计数和决策可能反映的是隐藏坐标选择而非模型明确定义的属性，建议采用正交不变的不变量测量方法。

Abstract: Hidden coordinates are not uniquely determined by a language model's input--output function, so representation-derived measurements should be invariant to function-preserving changes of basis. This study shows that column-permutation parallel analysis violates function-preserving reparameterization invariance because its reference distribution and selected component count can change while the model function and observed covariance spectrum remain fixed. More generally, a data-internal reference procedure cannot simultaneously preserve every coordinate marginal, remain orthogonally equivariant, and remove cross-coordinate covariance. Empirically, across five models, three retrieval domains, and 75 transformations, median component-count disagreement is 0.79 and median fixed-threshold decision disagreement is 0.26. A centering-only control isolates the reference-driven effect, with 1,141 of 1,200 component counts changing despite an unchanged observed spectrum, whereas independent parallel analysis seeds change none of the corresponding decisions. By contrast, orthogonally invariant comparator scores remain numerically stable with similar held-out discrimination. Together, these results show that parallel analysis-derived component counts and decisions can reflect hidden-coordinate choice rather than a well-defined property of the model.

</details>


### [230] [Active Diffusion-Based Inference for Ill-Posed Inverse Problems under Incomplete Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27080)
*Jitao Xu,Nobuo Sato,Yaohang Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于主动扩散模型的逆问题求解器，通过训练扩散模型学习参数空间与可观测空间的映射，并利用后验不确定性迭代检测和纠正模型失配，实现域自适应扩增，即使在初始训练范围排除真实参数的情况下也能鲁棒地推断逆问题解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学和工程应用中需要从实验观测数据估计未知参数，这是一个逆问题，但由于非线性、噪声和不适定性，求解本质困难。传统方法在初始先验/训练范围不包含真实参数时往往会失败，缺乏自适应的域扩增机制。

Method: 本文利用扩散模型(DM)训练学习参数空间与可观测空间之间的映射。通过迭代地检测和纠正模型失配（基于后验不确定性），方法能够发现并学习正确的参数空间区域，即使初始训练边界排除了真实参数。该方法为自适应域扩增提供了贝叶斯原则性的理论支撑。

Result: 在具有无限解的玩具逆问题以及量子色动力学(QCD)核子结构分析中，从量子关联函数参数化到事件可观测量上都展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于后验不确定性的主动扩散模型为逆问题提供了鲁棒的推断框架，能够在不完整先验知识条件下通过贝叶斯启发的自适应域扩增实现准确的参数估计。

Abstract: Many scientific and engineering applications require estimating unknown parameters from experimentally observable data -- an inverse problem that is inherently challenging due to nonlinearity, noise, and ill-posedness. In this paper, we propose an active diffusion-based inverse problem solver. A DM is trained to learn the mapping between the parameter space and the observable space. By iteratively detecting and correcting model misspecification through posterior uncertainty, the method discovers and learns the correct region of parameter space, even when initial training bounds exclude the true parameters. This provides a principled, Bayesian justification for adaptive domain augmentation and ensures robust inference for inverse problems under incomplete prior knowledge. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our inverse solver for a toy inverse problem with infinite solutions, and for the parameterization of the quantum correlation functions to event observables in a Quantum Chromodynamics analysis of nucleon structure.

</details>


### [231] [Recovering Expert Critic-Sourced Network Adjacency between Musical Artists from Acoustic Distributions: A Construct-Validity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27291)
*Elena Badillo-Goicoechea,Fengfeng He*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文论证了"评论性临近"（评论文本中评论家对艺术家配对关系）是一种比用户交互和音乐内容更丰富的推荐信号，并通过最优传输距离对声学内容进行外部验证，证明该信号在冷启动场景下具有可恢复性和construct validity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐推荐主要依赖用户-物品交互（冷启动时失效）和内在音乐内容两大信号。作者认为第三方信号——评论家在其长篇散文中明确关联两位艺术家的"评论性临近"关系——更为丰富且更具原则性，但此前只有内部效度验证，缺乏外部效度验证，即这种关系是否真正根植于音乐本身而非仅是社会文化语境。

Method: 将艺术家表示为80个Essentia声学描述符上的经验分布，利用边际最优传输（Wasserstein）距离建模配对邻近性，在艺术家不相交（artist-disjoint）的冷启动划分下评估评论性临近关系的可恢复性，并使用集成模型进行同外样本预测。

Result: 集成模型实现out-of-sample AUC 0.767（95% CI 0.761-0.775）；随评论共识度单调上升，多来源证实的边达到0.865。分层评估显示，边界紧凑、场景导向的流派（如scene-based genres）可恢复性高于宽泛的行业伞状术语流派。

Conclusion: 评论性话语是可分解为"可复现的声音核心"和"由叙事定位、亚文化语境及正典安置驱动的社会学剩余"的信息源，既提供了可扩展的冷启动发现机制，也为MIR（音乐信息检索）和MRS（音乐推荐系统）研究提供了社会学视角的方法论基础。

Abstract: Music recommendation relies primarily on two signals: user-item interactions, which fail in the cold-start regime, and intrinsic musical content, available for any recording. We argue that a third, largely untapped signal is both richer and more principled: critical adjacency, the pairwise relation established when an expert critic explicitly links two artists in long-form prose. It encodes deliberate judgments about which artists belong together. Prior work established its internal validity, showing it recovers coherent, interpretable communities and can match collaborative filtering in user-satisfaction simulations, with no user data. What has been missing is external validation: whether this critic-sourced relation is grounded in the music itself versus sociological context. We test it against acoustic content, reframing the question as one of construct validity.
  Representing artists as empirical distributions over 80 low-level Essentia acoustic descriptors and modeling pairwise proximity via marginal optimal-transport (Wasserstein) distances, we evaluate how far critical adjacency is sonically recoverable under a cold-start, artist-disjoint split. Our ensemble recovers these edges at out-of-sample AUC of 0.767 (95% CI 0.761-0.775). Recoverability rises monotonically with critical consensus, reaching 0.865 on multi-source attested edges. Stratified evaluations align with sociological models of genre: tightly bounded, scene-based genres show higher recoverability than broad industry umbrella terms.
  Critical discourse is thus a rich source of information for recommendation, decomposing into a reproducible "sonic core" and a "sociological remainder" driven by narrative positioning, subcultural context, and canonical placement. The work offers both a scalable cold-start discovery mechanism and a sociologically grounded approach to MIR and MRS research.

</details>


### [232] [A Finite Sample Analysis for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27313)
*Zijie Cheng,Xiang Li,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为表格型分布强化学习中的同步分位数时序差分学习（QTD）建立了全局有限样本保证，通过分离全局比较论证与局部线性化两种稳定性机制，证明了在特定步长下最后一次迭代的波动阶数为 $\widetilde O(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-\gamma})$，且不依赖分位数数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中的QTD算法是估计回报分布的重要方法，但此前缺乏全局有限样本收敛性分析。现有分析往往局限于局部收敛或在温和假设下成立，无法刻画从任意初始化的全局收敛行为，特别是无法同时捕捉随机波动与全局样本复杂度之间的区别。

Method: 证明分为两个稳定性机制：(1) 基于奖励累积分布函数的序单调性和分布Bellman算子的 $W_\infty$ 压缩性，通过全局比较论证将任意初始化的迭代吸引到局部邻域；(2) 在邻域内对QTD均值场进行线性化，其Jacobian为非奇异M-矩阵，利用对应的正半群进行方差敏感的鞅分析。

Result: 对步长 $\alpha_t=c(t+1)^{-a}$（$a\in(1/2,1)$），最后一次迭代的前导波动阶为 $\widetilde O(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-\gamma})$，且对分位数数量无多项式依赖。确定性瞬态和所需burn-in时间在最坏情形下依赖于Bellman目标密度（阶为 $m^{-1}$）。

Conclusion: 该结果清晰区分了局部随机波动与全局样本复杂度：局部波动的收敛速率是超快的且不依赖于分位数数目，而全局样本复杂度（瞬态和burn-in）在最坏情况下与最小Bellman目标密度相关（$m^{-1}$阶），揭示了QTD算法在不同层面的动力学特征。

Abstract: We establish a global finite-sample guarantee for synchronous quantile temporal-difference learning (QTD) in tabular distributional reinforcement learning. The proof separates two stability mechanisms. A global comparison argument, based on the order monotonicity of reward cumulative distribution functions and the $W_\infty$ contraction of the distributional Bellman operator, brings an arbitrarily initialized iterate into a local neighborhood. Inside that neighborhood, we linearize the QTD mean field. Its Jacobian is a nonsingular $M$-matrix, and the associated positive semigroup permits a variance-sensitive martingale analysis. For stepsizes $α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}$ with $a\in(1/2,1)$, the leading last-iterate fluctuation is of order $\widetilde O\bigl(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-γ}\bigr)$ and has no polynomial dependence on the number of quantiles. The deterministic transient and the required burn-in can still depend on the smallest Bellman-target density, which is of order $m^{-1}$ in the worst case. The result therefore distinguishes sharply between the local stochastic fluctuation and the global sample complexity.

</details>

### [233] [Dimension comparison for Student's statistic under symmetric unimodality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26421)
*Jacopo Lenzi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究自归一化均匀变量和的Student t统计量的Edgeworth展开，在临界值r=3处第一分布敏感项消失。作者在移动边界r_n=3+λ/n下比较n维与n-k维的展开，证明了第一个非零差值收敛到一个显式相位面H_δ(λ)，其零曲线统一了固定、次线性与固定比例删除三种情形，切线与12/35相交。通过Khintchine尺度混合表示，构造了一个与n无关的紧支撑C^∞对称单峰父分布，其Student尾概率在名义水平趋向2{1-Φ(√3)}（从下方）时对所有充分大的n超过等尺度均匀尾。相反，分位数比序显示均匀父分布使每个偶数矩及每个具有非负系数的收敛偶幂级数最大化。文章还推导了固定置信维数展开及n=6处的精确反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在渐近统计中，Student t统计量的Edgeworth展开在r=3处具有特殊性质——第一分布敏感项在此处消失，使得高阶比较变得可行。作者希望在此临界点及其邻域内系统地比较不同维数（n与n-k）下自归一化和的尾概率，从而揭示维数删除对分布的影响，并探索是否存在非均匀父分布在Student尾上超越均匀分布。

Method: 采用双边的Edgeworth展开技术在移动边界r_n=3+λ/n下进行渐近比较。利用Khintchine尺度混合表示将自归一化和转化为尺度混合结构。通过分位数比（quantile-ratio）序进行矩与幂级数的比较。推导固定置信维数展开，并对小样本（n=6）情形做精确分析。

Result: 在n与n-k维之间的第一个非零展开差收敛到显式相位面H_δ(λ)，其零曲线提供了统一框架，切线与12/35相交。构造了一个紧支撑C^∞对称单峰父分布，其Student尾超过等尺度均匀尾。均匀父分布在偶数矩及非负系数偶幂级数意义上是最优的。存在n=6处的精确反转现象。

Conclusion: 均匀分布在矩意义下是最优的，但在特定尾概率比较（如Student尾）下并非总是优势分布。临界点r=3附近的渐近行为由显式相位面完全刻画，且随着λ的变化存在零曲线穿越（对应均匀与替代分布之间的优势切换）。这些结果深化了对自归一化和在临界值的精细渐近结构的理解。

Abstract: Let $q_n(r)$ denote the tail probability at $r$ of the self-normalized sum of $n$ independent centered uniform variables. At $r=3$, the first distribution-sensitive term in the two-sided Edgeworth expansion of Student's statistic vanishes. We evaluate the expansion at the common moving boundary $r_n=3+λ/n$ in dimensions $n$ and $n-k$. Uniformly over deletion ranks retaining a fixed positive fraction of observations, the first nonzero difference converges to an explicit phase surface $H_δ(λ)$; its zero curve unifies fixed, sublinear and fixed-fraction deletions, with tangent crossing $12/35$. Through the Khintchine scale-mixture representation, this comparison yields a single compactly supported $C^\infty$ symmetric unimodal parent, independent of $n$, whose Student tail exceeds the equal-scale uniform tail for all sufficiently large $n$ along nominal levels tending to $2\{1-Φ(\sqrt3)\}$ from below. In contrast, a quantile-ratio order shows that the uniform parent maximizes every even moment and every convergent even power series with nonnegative coefficients. We also derive the fixed-confidence dimension expansion and an exact reversal at $n=6$.

</details>


### [234] [Posterior consistency for subdiffusion inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26536)
*Haoyu Lu,Shaokang Zu,Junxiong Jia*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究半线性时间分数阶次扩散方程中初值条件的贝叶斯反演问题。基于Whittle–Matérn过程的缩放高斯先验被赋予未知初值，作者证明了在非线性项满足Lipschitz条件时解的H^{2+κ}正则性，建立了预测误差在L^2范数下和后验参数在Sobolev范数下的后验收缩率，并通过构造小波填充集和控制KL散度证明了极小极大下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分数阶次扩散方程在反常扩散现象（如多孔介质、生物组织等）中广泛出现，其初值反演是一个重要的不适定逆问题。现有工作缺乏对半线性情形下贝叶斯方法的系统性收敛性分析，尤其是随机观测下的后验收缩率与极小极大下界尚未建立，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用基于Whittle–Matérn过程的缩放高斯先验对未知初值建模，先证明非线性项在H^κ范数下满足Lipschitz条件时解的H^{2+κ}正则性，再结合贝叶斯非参数方法推导后验收缩率；通过构造小波填充集（wavelet-packing set）并控制Kullback–Leibler散度来证明极小极大下界。

Result: 获得了预测误差在L^2范数下和参数在Sobolev范数下的后验收缩率，收缩率随样本量呈多项式衰减，且指数依赖于先验光滑度和空间维数；同时证明了相应的极小极大下界，表明所得收缩率在极小极大意义上是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文为半线性时间分数阶次扩散方程的贝叶斯初值反演提供了完整的理论框架，包括正则性分析、后验收缩率以及极小极大最优性保证，为该类问题在随机噪声观测下的贝叶斯推断奠定了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the Bayesian recovery of the initial state in a semilinear time-fractional subdiffusion equation from noisy random space-time point observations. A rescaled Gaussian prior based on a Whittle--Matérn process is assigned to the unknown initial condition. We prove the \(H^{2+κ}\)-regularity of the solution when the nonlinearity satisfies a Lipschitz condition in the \(H^κ\)-norm. We then establish posterior contraction rates for the prediction error in the \(L^2\)-norm and for the parameter in Sobolev norms. The rates are polynomial in the sample size, with exponent depending on the prior smoothness and the spatial dimension. Moreover, we prove a minimax lower bound by constructing a wavelet-packing set and controlling the Kullback--Leibler divergences.

</details>


### [235] [How far can symmetry help? Phase transitions and symmetry selection in sparse functional data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27055)
*Jocelyn Nembe*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏函数型数据中协方差估计的相变现象，揭示了定义域对称性如何改变阈值。存在q阶循环对称性时，采样点数阈值从经典值n^{1/(2β)}降至n^{1/(2β)}q^{-1/2}，并存在饱和效应：任何旋转对称（即使完整圆群）也无法将阈值降至n^{1/(4β)}以下。对称性在经典阈值与常数采样之间的对数尺度上起到"走到一半"的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏函数型数据分析中，协方差面的估计存在从二维非参数速率到参数速率n^{-1}的相变，阈值点为m*_n ≍ n^{1/(2β)}。一个自然且重要的开放问题是：定义域的对称性是否会、以及如何改变这一相变阈值。该问题在理论和应用层面都有意义，因为许多实际问题中的域（如圆、球面）天然具有对称性。

Method: 将对称性建模为作用在过程与设计上的q阶循环群。利用对称性对"可用配对数目"的效应（进入方差为二次形式，故指数取平方根）推导阈值位移；通过泊松求和与核自相关的正定性证明饱和效应，使约减因子为min(q, c_K/h)。提出利用对称性的估计器，并构造保持正定性的驻留子类打包以建立均匀下界。对近似对称情形，在傅里叶坐标下显式刻画对称谱，并用hold-out程序在饱和前取前导常数一、饱和后取绝对常数内的对称水平。

Result: 对称性将阈值从n^{1/(2β)}降至n^{1/(2β)}q^{-1/2}（对完整圆群即降至n^{1/(4β)}的极限），饱和后速率塌缩为一维非参数速率。估计器达到此下界（从上方），下界与之匹配至多项式因子。近似对称情形下设计平面分裂为三个区域，其中一区无法通过增加采样触及。所有结论均经数值验证。

Conclusion: 对称性在经典阈值与常数采样之间的对数尺度上提供"一半"的改善（n^{1/(2β)} → n^{1/(4β)}），但无法进一步降低；对称性通过增加有效配对数目起作用，而参数速率固有的地板不受群平均影响。域的重参数化不改变阈值。均匀下界与上界之间存在一个多项式因子的间隙，闭合该间隙仍是开放问题。

Abstract: In sparse functional data analysis, where $n$ curves are each observed at $m$ random points, the covariance surface undergoes a sharp phase transition: if the covariance has smoothness $β$, the risk drops from the two-dimensional nonparametric rate to the parametric rate $n^{-1}$ once $m$ exceeds $m^*_n \asymp n^{1/(2β)}$. We determine what a symmetry of the domain does to that transition. A cyclic group of order $q$ preserving process and design displaces the threshold to $n^{1/(2β)}q^{-1/2}$; the exponent is a square root because symmetry acts on the number of usable pairs, which enters the variance quadratically, while the parametric floor is untouched by group averaging. The displacement saturates: once the orbit is finer than the bandwidth the reduction factor is $\min(q,c_K/h)$, by Poisson summation and positive definiteness of the kernel autocorrelation, and beyond that point the rate collapses to the one-dimensional nonparametric rate. Hence no rotation symmetry, even the full circle group, lowers the threshold below $n^{1/(4β)}$; this floor is attained from above by our estimator and from below, up to a polynomial factor, by our lower bounds. Symmetry thus takes one halfway on a logarithmic scale from the classical threshold to constant sampling. The uniform lower bound rests on a positivity-preserving packing of the stationary sub-class; closing the gap is left open. If the symmetry is only approximate, the risk acquires an approximation term and the design plane splits into three regimes, one unreachable by additional sampling; the symmetry spectrum governing it is explicit in Fourier coordinates, and a hold-out procedure selects the symmetry level with leading constant one below saturation and within an absolute constant beyond. All laws above are confirmed numerically. Reparametrisation of the domain, by contrast, leaves the threshold unchanged.

</details>


### [236] [Connecting Riemannian Geometry and Statistical Inference for Correlation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27209)
*Argyn Kuketayev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文揭示了商仿射度量（quotient-affine metric）与Jennrich 1970年渐近检验之间的深层联系：Jennrich统计量中的二次型恰好是商仿射度量张量的一半，并证明了在独立高斯样本且具有共同总体相关矩阵的条件下，放缩后的测地线距离平方依分布收敛于4χ²_d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商仿射度量赋予满秩相关矩阵内在黎曼几何结构，但其测地线距离缺乏闭式解，也缺少解析的渐近零分布。作者希望建立该几何与已有经典统计检验（Jennrich检验）的关联，从而为相关矩阵的假设检验提供理论支持。

Method: 作者通过将消除边际标准差的过程与商掉对角重标定（diagonal rescaling）操作相联系，揭示了高斯Fisher信息中的投影过程与商仿射度量的商构造之间的对应关系。基于此恒等式，推导出Jennrich统计量可直接评估局部商仿射二次型，并进一步推导出测地线距离平方的渐近分布。

Result: 对于两个独立高斯样本且具有共同总体相关矩阵的情形，测地线距离平方乘以有效样本量后依分布收敛于4χ²_d，其中d=p(p-1)/2。当p=2时，该结果退化为两样本Fisher z检验。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了商仿射几何与经典相关矩阵检验之间的理论桥梁，为相关矩阵的假设检验提供了基于黎曼几何的全新视角，并给出了测地线距离的解析渐近分布，弥补了此前仅有数值方法的不足。

Abstract: The quotient-affine metric gives an intrinsic Riemannian geometry to full-rank correlation matrices, but its geodesic distance has no closed form and we are not aware of an analytic asymptotic null distribution for it. We connect this geometry, introduced in 2019, with Jennrich's 1970 asymptotic test for equality of correlation matrices. The quadratic form underlying Jennrich's statistic is exactly one half of the quotient-affine metric tensor. The identity arises because eliminating marginal standard deviations from Gaussian Fisher information performs the same projection as quotienting out diagonal rescalings. Jennrich's statistic therefore evaluates the local quotient-affine quadratic form directly. Moreover, for two independent Gaussian samples with a common population correlation matrix, the squared geodesic distance, scaled by effective sample size, converges in distribution to $4χ^2_d$, where $d = p(p-1)/2$. For $p=2$, the result reduces to the two-sample Fisher $z$ test.

</details>


### [237] [Conformal Prediction Through the Lens of Hypothesis Testing: Universality, Impossibility, and Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27310)
*Ryan J. Tibshirani,Rina Foygel Barber,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种看待共形预测的新视角：不是从检验H0:Y_{n+1}=y的置换检验出发，而是从n+1个样本联合分布的交换性零假设出发，通过置换检验的反演来构建共形预测集。这一视角更贴近经典假设检验的正规化框架，使研究者能够利用Neyman、Lehmann、Scheffé、Kraft、Le Cam等经典检验理论直接复现共形预测文献中的普适性和不可能性结果，并通过标准Neyman-Pearson理论推导出共形预测的最优性结果——最优预测集方法是以Y|X的逆条件密度作为得分函数的共形预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管共形预测与置换检验之间的联系已广为人知，但现有动机视角（检验Y_{n+1}=y的假设）在形式化上与经典假设检验理论不够贴近。作者希望从一个不同的、更符合传统假设检验形式化的视角（检验n+1个样本联合分布的交换性）来审视共形预测，从而能够将经典检验理论中的丰富成果直接应用于共形预测领域，为已知结果提供更简洁的推导，并挖掘新的最优性结论。

Method: 作者将共形预测重新形式化为置换检验（零假设为n+1个样本联合分布的交换性）的反演。利用假设检验与置信集之间的对偶性，将共形预测文献中的基础普适性和不可能性结果映射到经典假设检验理论（Neyman、Lehmann、Scheffé、Kraft、Le Cam等）中直接推导。同时运用标准Neyman-Pearson理论，在交换性分布的有效覆盖约束下推导最优预测集方法，证明以Y|X的逆条件密度作为得分的共形预测器是最优的。

Result: 作者成功表明：(1) 共形预测的普适性和不可能性结果可以仅使用经典假设检验理论直接复现；(2) 通过Neyman-Pearson理论可以推导出共形预测的最优性结果——对于任意协变量与响应的联合分布X,Y及任意样本量，在所有对交换性分布具有有效覆盖的预测集方法中，以Y|X的逆条件密度为得分函数的共形预测器产生最高效（最紧凑）的预测集。

Conclusion: 以交换性零假设为出发点的置换检验反演视角，比传统的Y_{n+1}=y检验视角更严格地符合经典假设检验的形式化框架，从而为共形预测理论提供了一条更直接且统一的推导路径。该视角不仅揭示了共形预测与经典检验理论之间的深层联系，还确立了共形预测在最优性方面的一个精确结果，表明密度比得分可以达到最优预测效率。

Abstract: The connections between conformal prediction and permutation tests are already widely-known in the literature. Some authors motivate conformal prediction by saying that it computes a permutation p-value for the hypothesis $H_0 : Y_{n+1} = y$, and then inverts this to form a prediction set for $Y_{n+1}$ (i.e., accepts all values $y$ into the prediction set for which the p-value is large). In this paper, we examine an alternative view, which is less well-known: we again cast conformal prediction via the inversion of a permutation test, but for the null of exchangeability of the joint distribution of the $n+1$ samples. This change in perspective, while simple, adheres more closely to traditional formalization in hypothesis testing, which offers several benefits. First, we use the duality between conformal sets and testing to show that foundational universality and impossibility results in the conformal prediction literature can be reproduced directly using classical hypothesis testing theory (due to Neyman, Lehmann, Scheffé, Kraft, Le Cam, and others). Furthermore, we show that an optimality result for conformal prediction can be derived using standard Neyman-Pearson theory: for any joint distribution of the covariates and response $X,Y$, and any sample size, the optimal method for prediction sets---which delivers the most efficient set among all methods with valid coverage for exchangeable distributions---is a conformal predictor whose score is the inverse conditional density of $Y|X$.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz: The Smoothing Benefit in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明Muon优化器中有限阶Newton-Schulz迭代不仅不是近似误差，反而对非光滑非凸优化有益。通过在线到非凸转换分析，证明了对数深度的Newton-Schulz迭代足以保证收敛到驻点，而使用精确极分解的Muon反而可能无法收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大语言模型预训练中是强大的矩阵参数优化器，但现有理论要么用精确极分解替代Newton-Schulz迭代，要么将其视为近似误差，导致理论上Muon实际运行的有限深度迭代只会损害收敛保证。这与Muon在实践中表现优异的事实相矛盾，需要解释有限Newton-Schulz迭代为何不仅无害反而有益。

Method: 通过在线到非凸转换框架分析Muon：将更新规则视为在线学习器，其遗憾界可转化为驻点保证。关键洞见是有限Newton-Schulz迭代将不连续的极映射平滑化为关于奇异值的Lipschitz映射，从而Muon可被视为具有平滑谱势的在线学习器。证明了Newton-Schulz深度仅需随目标精度对数增长即可保证非光滑非凸优化的收敛性，而精确极分解更新可能失败。

Result: 样本复杂度下界与已知最优的非光滑非凸优化保证相匹配，且在光滑非凸优化中达到问题相关因子意义下的最优性。论证可推广至具有相同平滑性质的一般谱映射。

Conclusion: 有限Newton-Schulz迭代的平滑效应是Muon在非光滑非凸优化中成功的关键，理论上优于精确极分解版本，并为谱映射类优化器提供了统一的分析框架。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for the matrix-valued parameters in large language model pretraining, approximately orthogonalizing its momentum with a few Newton-Schulz iterations. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. To this end, we analyze Muon through the online-to-nonconvex conversion, which views the update rule as an online learner and converts its regret bound into a stationarity guarantee. The finite Newton-Schulz iteration smooths the discontinuous polar map into a Lipschitz map of the singular values, and Muon with finite Newton-Schulz can be regarded as an online learner with a smoothed spectral potential. This smoothing is exactly what the conversion needs: we prove that a Newton-Schulz depth growing only logarithmically in the target accuracy suffices for convergence to stationary points in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, whereas Muon with the exact-polar update may fail to converge. The resulting sample complexity bounds match the best-known guarantees for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and are optimal for smooth nonconvex optimization up to problem-dependent factors. The argument extends beyond Newton-Schulz to general spectral maps with the same smoothing property.

</details>


### [239] [Bayesian methods and Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithms for curve reconstruction and point cloud data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26490)
*Asir Intesar Tushar,Ioannis Sgouralis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全贝叶斯框架，用于表示点云数据并重建闭合曲线，将观测点建模为潜在位置（受限于底层曲线）的噪声扰动，并通过非参数先验进行正则化，利用定制的MCMC采样器进行后验推断，在合成数据和真实LiDAR数据集上展示了精确重建和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云数据分析面临数据量大、定位噪声和缺失信息等挑战；现有重建流程通常仅返回单一最佳拟合结构，缺乏不确定性量化，无法完整描述重建结果的可靠性。

Method: 构建完全贝叶斯框架：观测点被建模为潜在位置（受限于底层闭合曲线）的噪声扰动，底层曲线由非参数先验进行正则化；后验推断通过一系列针对点云特性定制的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样器完成。

Result: 在合成示例和真实LiDAR数据集上的数值实验表明，该框架能实现精确的曲线重建，并对恢复的曲线提供量化不确定性。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架为点云闭合曲线重建提供了同时具备精度和不确定性量化的解决方案，克服了传统单一结构输出的局限。

Abstract: Point-cloud data routinely captured by modern imaging and sensor technologies provide detailed geometric descriptions of objects and environments, but their analysis is hindered by large data volumes, localization noise, and missing information. In addition, existing point-cloud reconstruction pipelines typically return a single best-fit structure without uncertainty quantification. We introduce a fully Bayesian framework for representing point-cloud data and reconstructing closed curves, in which observed points are modeled as noisy perturbations of latent locations constrained to lie on the underlying curve that is regularized by a non-parametric prior. Posterior inference in our framework is carried out using a series of Markov chain Monte Carlo samplers tailored to point-cloud characteristics. Numerical experiments, including synthetic examples and real-world LiDAR datasets, show accurate reconstructions and quantified uncertainty over the recovered curves.

</details>


### [240] [Algorithmic Principles For Multiclass Learning Are Hard To Come By: Limits of Regularization and Proper Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26516)
*Julian Asilis,Shaddin Dughmi,Vatsal Sharan,Alec Sun,Shang-Hua Teng,Chang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回答了统计学习理论中两个核心问题——学习能否归结为适当学习（proper learning），以及适当/非适当多类学习能否被正则化器所刻画——并给出了否定的答案，解决了先前工作中提出的三个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习理论中最基本的问题是如何刻画可学习性问题以及如何构建学习算法。虽然可学习性的刻画（如组合维度）已有优雅答案，但普遍适用的多类学习器都依赖复杂度极高的单包含结构（one-inclusion structure）方向选择。先前的理论工作暗示学习可能归结为适当学习或可通过正则化来理解，本文试图验证这些猜想。

Method: 通过构造反例和精确刻画来回答三个开放问题：（1）构造一个可学习的多类问题，它无法嵌入任何适当可学习的假设类；（2）精确定义适当学习所需的训练误差规模——证明每个适当可学习类都存在样本大小为m时仅犯o(m)次错误的适当学习器，但任意预先给定的次线性尺度a_m=o(m)都是某个适当可学习问题的必要条件；（3）构造反例证明结构风险最小化（SRM）和局部正则化器都无法作为通用学习算法。同时给出SRM可学习性和正则化器可表示性的充分条件及刻画。

Result: 三个否定性结果：（1）存在可学习但不能通过扩大假设类归结为适当学习的多类问题；（2）适当学习的训练误差具有精确的复杂度刻画——o(m)是上界且不可改进到任何特定次线性尺度；（3）SRM对适当可学习类不是通用的，局部正则化器对所有可学习类也不是通用的。正面结果方面，给出了SRM可学习性的两个充分条件，并通过揭示偏好（revealed preferences）的可积性刻画了SRM可表示性。

Conclusion: 学习不能普遍地归结为适当学习，适当学习和正则化方法均有其固有局限性。这些否定结果表明，已知的通用多类学习器所依赖的复杂结构可能是不可避免的。正面理论则为理解何种情况下SRM和正则化方法有效提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Two of the most fundamental questions in statistical learning theory are the following: which prediction problems are learnable, and how should they be learned? For the former, elegant answers often take the form of combinatorial dimensions. The latter question, however, has proved considerably more elusive: all known general-purpose multiclass learners rely on intricate orientations of exponentially large one-inclusion structures, and familiar algorithmic principles such as proper learning and regularization remain poorly understood. Motivated by prior work, we ask whether learning reduces to proper learning---possibly over a larger hypothesis class---and whether proper or improper multiclass learning can ultimately be captured by suitable regularizers.
  Our primary results answer both questions negatively, resolving three open problems from prior work. First, we exhibit a learnable multiclass problem that cannot be embedded in any properly learnable class, meaning learning cannot be reduced to proper learning by enlarging the hypothesis class. Second, we demonstrate that proper learning can require training error and characterize this phenomenon precisely: every properly learnable class admits a proper learner making $o(m)$ errors on samples of size $m$, but every prescribed sublinear scale $a_m=o(m)$ is necessary for some properly learnable problem. Third, regularization is not a general learner: we exhibit a properly learnable class that cannot be learned by any Structural Risk Minimization (SRM) learner, and a learnable class that cannot be learned by any local regularizer. We complement these impossibility results with a positive theory that gives two sufficient conditions for SRM learnability and characterizes SRM representability through integrability of revealed preferences.

</details>


### [241] [GRAS: Guided Reduced-Variance Proposals and Adaptive Selection for Training-Free Reward Alignment in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26585)
*Kwanyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAS是一种训练自由的离散扩散模型引导方法，通过Rao-Blackwellized揭示和leave-one-out基线降低梯度估计方差，并通过组相对优势实现自适应重采样温度，在DNA和蛋白质设计任务上达到最佳训练自由奖励，甚至可匹配或超越经过奖励微调的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型是序列数据生成的主流方法，但在推理时无需重训练即可引导其朝向下游奖励是日益重要的需求。现有训练自由引导方法结合梯度引导与搜索时存在两个弱点：引导提议从单个噪声样本估计梯度导致方差大，以及搜索以固定温度重采样粒子忽略了奖励在不同去噪步骤间的分布差异。

Method: GRAS包含两类改进：（1）提议端：对于可微奖励采用Rao-Blackwellized reveal降低估计器方差；对于不可微奖励采用leave-one-out基线。（2）搜索端：将每步值标准化为组相对优势，并证明其最终可归结为一个关键成分——自适应重采样温度。所有改动均不增加去噪器计算成本。

Result: 在调控DNA和蛋白质设计任务上，GRAS达到最佳训练自由奖励，优于先前的训练自由方法，并匹配或超越奖励微调模型；即使在不可微奖励场景下依然保持有效性。

Conclusion: GRAS以简单的方式同时修复了梯度引导与搜索两个环节的缺陷，证明训练自由的离散扩散引导可以通过低方差的提议和自适应重采样温度达到与奖励微调相当的性能，为训练自由引导范式提供了更强的基础。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have become a strong, widely adopted class of generators for sequence data, and steering them toward a downstream reward at inference time, without any retraining, is increasingly important. Such training-free steering is done by gradient guidance, by search, or by combining the two. We study the combined regime and identify two weaknesses in how it is usually run: the guided proposal estimates its gradient from a single noisy sample, and the search then resamples particles at a fixed temperature that ignores how rewards spread across each denoising step. We address both with a small set of changes that add no denoiser cost. For the proposal, we lower the estimator variance with a Rao-Blackwellized reveal for differentiable rewards and a leave-one-out baseline for non-differentiable ones; for the search, we standardize the per-step values into a group-relative advantage and prove it collapses to a single active ingredient, an adaptive resampling temperature. We call the resulting method Guided Reduced-variance proposals and Adaptive Selection (GRAS). GRAS is simple yet effective: across regulatory DNA and protein design it attains the best training-free reward, outperforming prior training-free methods and matching or surpassing a reward-fine-tuned model, and it remains effective even for non-differentiable rewards.

</details>


### [242] [When Is the Sharp Covariance Envelope Tight? Feature-Only Geometry for Volume-Sampled Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26877)
*Kihun Rhee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了普通索引固定规模体积抽样下、选定未加权最小二乘的中心系数协方差的Loewner包络，并给出了精确的谱相变条件，建立了从特征边际nu_A到协方差包络严格性之间等价刻画的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Derezinski和Warmuth的工作确立了体积抽样的全规模采样恒等式、selected-OLS无偏性及逆矩，但其精确的固定响应损失与预测协方差公式仅局限于秩-规模端点s=d。本文旨在将这些结果推广到任意合法预算d <= s <= m的全秩固定池情形，填补中等预算下的理论空白。

Method: 采用普通索引固定规模体积抽样配合选定未加权最小二乘；通过残差增强的测度变换提供响应感知机制和单侧定量松弛界；利用支撑饱和性证明可达性方向；用临界等杠杆几何解释边界；通过冻结特征实例给出非空上证书并度量固定池代价。

Result: 建立了对任意全秩固定池、响应及合法预算的中心系数协方差Loewner包络，其系数在全秩类上全局尖锐；证明了nu_A>0当且仅当归一化谱包络对每个相容残差严格，nu_A=0当且仅当存在谱紧的相容残差且该残差在所有严格内部预算下保持紧性；给出保守的同原始基数决策下界证书。

Conclusion: 本文的结论仅涉及条件中心化的、全Gram白化的系数协方差，而非总体泛化性质；全局尖锐性不决定在手池上的可达性，需结合特征边际nu_A进行精确判定。

Abstract: Prior analyses by Derezinski and Warmuth established all-size sampling identities, selected-OLS unbiasedness, and inverse moments for ordinary volume sampling, while their exact arbitrary-fixed-response loss and prediction-covariance formulas are at the rank-size endpoint s=d. We establish a Loewner envelope for centered coefficient covariance for every full-rank fixed pool, response, and legal budget d <= s <= m under ordinary indexed fixed-size volume sampling followed by selected unweighted least squares; its coefficient is globally sharp over the full-rank class. Global sharpness does not determine attainability on the pool in hand. Under positive loss, strict-interior budgets, and no coloops, a feature-only margin nu_A gives the exact fixed-design spectral phase: nu_A > 0 if and only if the normalized spectral envelope is strict for every compatible residual, whereas nu_A = 0 if and only if some compatible residual is spectrally tight; the same zero-margin residual is tight at every strict-interior budget. A residual-augmented change of measure supplies the response-aware mechanism and a one-sided quantitative slack bound, while support saturation proves the attainment direction. Critical equal-leverage geometry interprets the boundary, and sound lower certificates yield conservative same-primitive cardinality decisions. Frozen-feature examples show that the certificate is nonvacuous and measure the fixed-pool cost of its authorized reduction. The claims concern conditional centered, full-Gram-whitened coefficient covariance, not population generalization.

</details>


### [243] [Gromov-Monge Flow Matching for Equivariant Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26961)
*Moritz Piening,Christian Wald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构感知的配对（structure-aware coupling）方法用于图上的流匹配（flow matching）生成模型。作者从理论上证明，图在节点置换下不变时，源-目标配对应在地图商空间（graph quotient space）中进行，其欧氏商度量等价于Gromov-Monge距离。由于精确对齐不可行，他们使用高效Gromov-Wasserstein类松弛构造小批量配对，该方法仅改变训练配对方式，兼容标准置换等变架构。实验表明这种方法在连续图生成和分子生成任务中显著提升小积分预算下的样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配模型虽然采用置换等变架构以利用图的置换不变性，但对称性同样应体现在源-目标配对中。当图对仅在节点重标记下可比时，自然的Wasserstein几何位于图商空间，其度量即Gromov-Monge距离。这揭示了当前方法在配对层面忽略了图的对称结构，作者希望通过理论分析和实用算法弥补这一空白。

Method: 1) 理论上证明商空间配对可以无损提升为对齐的代表元（aligned representatives），且对称化可得到等变流匹配最小化器，含类别端点预测场景；2) 由于精确Gromov-Monge对齐计算不可行，使用Gromov-Wasserstein类松弛和节点对齐下界构造小批量配对的耦合，可结合外部图间分配；3) 该方法只修改训练耦合，保持标准置换等变架构不变。

Result: 在连续图生成和类别型分子生成任务上，结构感知配对在小积分预算下显著提升了样本质量；大规模分子模型在传统多步采样下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 将图的对称性纳入训练耦合而非仅依赖架构的置换等变性，能够有效改善流匹配在图上生成任务中的效率与质量，尤其在采样预算受限时优势明显，且与现有等变架构无缝兼容。

Abstract: Graphs are invariant under node permutations, motivating the use of permutation-equivariant architectures in generative models. In flow matching, however, symmetry may also enter the source--target coupling: once graph pairs are compared up to node relabeling, the natural Wasserstein geometry is that of the graph quotient space. The Euclidean quotient metric of this space coincides with the Gromov--Monge distance, obtained by optimally relabeling the nodes. We develop this perspective theoretically, showing that quotient couplings can be lifted to aligned representatives without additional cost and that symmetrization yields equivariant flow-matching minimizers, including for categorical endpoint prediction. In practice, exact Gromov--Monge alignment is intractable, so we construct minibatch couplings using efficient Gromov--Wasserstein-type relaxations and lower bounds for the inner node alignment, optionally combined with an outer assignment between graphs. The resulting procedure changes only the training coupling and is compatible with standard permutation-equivariant architectures. Across continuous graph and categorical molecular generation, these structure-aware couplings substantially improve sample quality at small integration budgets, while our scaled-up molecular models remain competitive under conventional many-step sampling.

</details>


### [244] [TRACE-CRC: Trajectory-Adaptive Conformal Risk Control for Multi-Step Channel State Information Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27124)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Mehdi Sattari,Javad Aliakbari,Tommy Svensson,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE-CRC，一种面向多步CSI预测的轨迹感知不确定性量化方法，通过轨迹自适应校准与误差画像结合共形风险控制，在保证轨迹级覆盖的同时显著缩小不确定球体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习CSI预测器仅提供点预测，缺乏校准的不确定性估计。在多步CSI预测中，目标是一系列未来CSI矩阵序列，任一帧预测不可靠都可能导致波束成形或调度等下游决策失败，因此亟需轨迹级的不确定性量化方法。

Method: TRACE-CRC围绕未来CSI预测矩阵构建Frobenius范数不确定球，并控制至少有一个未来帧未被覆盖的风险（即轨迹级覆盖）。方法融合三个方面：未来步相关的误差画像（error profiling）、轨迹难度分层（trajectory difficulty stratification）以及learn-then-test（LTT）风险控制，而非对每个未来步独立校准。

Result: 实验表明TRACE-CRC实现了可靠的轨迹级覆盖，与保守的多步校正方法相比具有明显更小的不确定球，同时避免了紧凑逐步法和自适应共形基线因覆盖不足而导致的轨迹欠覆盖问题。

Conclusion: TRACE-CRC是一种有效的轨迹感知不确定性量化框架，在保证多步CSI预测轨迹级可靠性的同时提升了不确定性估计的紧凑性与效率。

Abstract: Reliable prediction of time-varying channel state information (CSI) is essential for efficient wireless communication. Each CSI frame is a matrix-valued representation of the wireless channel response, and a sequence of CSI frames forms a temporal channel trajectory. Modern deep learning-based CSI predictors, however, often provide only point predictions and lack calibrated uncertainty estimates. This limitation is particularly problematic in multi-step CSI prediction, where the target is a sequence of future CSI matrices, and downstream decisions such as beamforming or scheduling may fail if any part of the predicted trajectory is unreliable. We propose trajectory-adaptive calibration and error profiling with conformal risk control (TRACE-CRC), a method for trajectory-aware uncertainty quantification in multi-step CSI prediction. TRACE-CRC constructs Frobenius-norm uncertainty balls around predicted CSI matrices and controls the risk that at least one future frame is uncovered. Instead of calibrating each future step independently, TRACE-CRC combines future-step-dependent error profiling, trajectory difficulty stratification, and learn-then-test (LTT) risk control. Empirically, TRACE-CRC achieves reliable trajectory-level coverage with substantially smaller uncertainty balls than conservative multi-step corrections, while avoiding the trajectory undercoverage of compact stepwise and adaptive conformal baselines.

</details>


### [245] [SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing for Labeled Property Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26132)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing架构，将小语言模型直接集成到图神经网络的图消息选择机制中，通过目标条件化的路由查询在关系类型内和跨关系级别选择相关消息，实现对异质标签属性图的语义感知学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络将节点和边的属性表示为静态特征向量，无法动态判断哪些语义证据应影响特定预测目标的消息传播，限制了在带丰富异质文本和分类属性的标签属性图上的学习能力。

Method: 采用双分支架构：拓扑GNN提供稳定的结构表示和预测锚点；每个目标节点的入射消息由邻居结构状态、节点属性编码、关系属性编码和关系类型组成；参数高效的SLM处理结构化图软token生成目标条件化路由查询，先在关系类型内选择相关消息，再跨关系级摘要路由信息；最终表示作为拓扑锚点的有界残差更新。

Result: 架构支持在邻居和关系类型级别的可解释分析，提供将语言派生语义集成到属性丰富图学习中的通用机制。

Conclusion: 该架构通过SLM条件化的分层关系路由，在不破坏结构证据的前提下，让上下文语义信息动态影响预测，为属性丰富图学习提供了新的可解释范式。

Abstract: Labeled property graphs combine relational structure with heterogeneous textual and categorical properties attached to both nodes and relationships. Conventional graph neural networks typically represent these properties as static feature vectors, limiting their ability to determine which semantic evidence should influence message propagation for a particular prediction target. We propose SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing, an architecture that integrates a small language model directly into graph message selection. A topology GNN provides a stable structural representation and prediction anchor. For each target node, incident messages combine the neighbor's structural state, node-property encoding, relationship-property encoding, and relationship type. A parameter-efficient SLM processes structured graph soft tokens and produces a target-conditioned routing query. This query first selects relevant messages within each relationship type and subsequently routes information across relation-level summaries. The resulting representation provides a bounded residual update to the topology anchor, preserving structural evidence while allowing contextual semantic information to modify the prediction. The architecture supports interpretable analysis at both the neighbor and relationship-type levels and provides a general mechanism for integrating language-derived semantics into property-rich graph learning.

</details>


### [246] [NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26222)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Muhammad Firhard Roslan,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuronFuzz，一个利用LLM内部安全神经元作为连续执行反馈的白盒模糊测试框架，用于LLM安全评估。它通过在prefill阶段获取可微分的连续安全警报分数，消除了响应生成的需求，并使用梯度引导模板变异，在21个文本和多模态模型上实现高达100%的越狱发现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化安全测试方法主要依赖响应级反馈，每个候选提示都需要生成目标模型的响应来评估攻击效果，这不仅成本高昂，而且在强对齐模型上只能提供稀疏的指导（大多数候选被拒绝且产生相同的失败结果），难以有效引导攻击优化。

Method: NeuronFuzz提出SafetyOracle，将安全神经元的激活转换为连续的警报分数作为模糊测试反馈，可在prefill阶段获取而无需生成响应。SafetyOracle通过模板不变的有害/良性输入和稳定性感知选择来识别紧凑的安全神经元集合。由于警报分数可微分，NeuronFuzz利用梯度识别安全敏感模板位置，并用掩码语言模型生成流畅、上下文兼容的变异，同时保留原始有害载荷并避免额外优化变量。

Result: 在5个白盒源模型上达到76-100%的越狱发现率，优于基线最多48个百分点；优化后的模板零样本迁移到开源权重和6个专有目标模型，平均ASR分别为69.6%（top-5）和92.6%（ensemble），EASR分别为44.1%和60.0%。实验覆盖21个文本和多模态模型。

Conclusion: NeuronFuzz通过利用安全神经元作为连续反馈信号，有效解决了传统响应级反馈成本高、指导稀疏的问题，显著提升了LLM安全评估的效率和有效性，并且生成的攻击模板具有良好的跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [247] [Pruning Binarized Neural Networks: A Dedicated Framework and Globally Weighted Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26233)
*Roan Rubiales,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于PyTorch的研究导向框架，用于设计和优化二值化神经网络，支持冻结与剪枝机制。基于该框架提出了一种考虑跨抽象层参数相对重要性的新型全局加权剪枝方法，在VGG11上实现70%剪枝率且精度不变，远超现有二值化设置下41%的最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的极端压缩（包括完全二值化）可大幅降低内存占用和算术复杂度，便于在FPGA和微控制器等边缘硬件上部署。然而，现有的剪枝策略不适用于二值化表示，难以转化为实际的硬件资源节省，因此需要专门针对二值化神经网络的剪枝方法和研究框架。

Method: 本文提出了一个PyTorch研究框架，集成了冻结（freezing）和剪枝（pruning）机制。在此框架基础上，提出了一种新型剪枝方法，该方法考量学习参数在不同抽象层级上的相对重要性，通过全局加权机制在不同剪枝率下实现精度与剪枝率之间的更优权衡。

Result: 该方法在VGG11上实现了70%的剪枝率且精度保持不变，而当前最优的二值化设置结果仅能达到41%，在剪枝率与精度的权衡上取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 通过全局加权剪枝机制，该框架与方法能够更高效地实现二值化神经网络的压缩，为边缘硬件上的高效部署提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Extreme compression of deep neural networks, up to full binarization, dramatically reduces memory footprint and arithmetic complexity, facilitating deployment on constrained edge hardware with field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) and microcontrollers. Although combining binarization with pruning promises additional efficiency gains, existing pruning strategies are ill-suited to binarized representations and rarely translate into meaningful hardware savings. We introduce a PyTorch-based, research-oriented framework that incorporates freezing and pruning mechanisms for designing and optimizing binarized neural networks. The framework enables rapid and reproducible evaluation of state-of-the-art approaches and the fast prototyping of new ones. Leveraging this framework, we propose a novel pruning method that accounts for the relative importance of learned parameters across abstraction levels. Such a global weighting mechanism consistently achieves a superior trade-off between model accuracy and pruning rate, achieving a 70% pruning rate on VGG11 with constant accuracy, while state-of-the-art results reach only 41% in the binarized setting.

</details>


### [248] [Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26309)
*Antonia van Betteray,Jonathan Klees,Miriam Schäfers,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMELS框架，通过快速构建邻域图和代数多重网格求解器，实现大规模高维数据集上的高效标签传播（Label Spreading）半监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型依赖大量标注数据，但人工标注大规模数据集成本高昂且耗时。标签传播作为半监督学习技术可以有效缓解这一问题，但它在大规模高维数据集上的应用受限于计算成本和内存约束。

Method: AMELS框架包含两个核心改进：(1) 快速构建邻域图以提升可扩展性；(2) 引入代数多重网格（AMG）迭代求解器，替代标签传播中常用的随机游走迭代。由于AMG的多层特性，AMELS可以在单个多重网格循环中在任意大小的图上传播标签信息。

Result: 实验证明AMELS相比现有实现显著降低了运行时间，同时对超参数选择更鲁棒（在运行时间和分类精度两方面）。在大规模图像数据集上，即使只有少量标注样本也能产生准确的标签。

Conclusion: AMELS框架使得标签传播能够高效地应用于大规模图像数据集，在仅有少量标注样本的情况下仍能获得准确的标签结果，显著提升了标签传播技术的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models rely on large amounts of labeled data. However, manual annotation of large-scale datasets is expensive and time-consuming. Label spreading is a semi-supervised learning technique that addresses this challenge by propagating information from a few labeled examples to a larger pool of unlabeled data. Despite its effectiveness, its application to large-scale, high-dimensional datasets is limited by computational costs and memory constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading (AMELS), an efficient label spreading framework that improves scalability by fast construction of neighborhood graphs and the incorporation of algebraic multigrid solvers. The latter is an iterative solver that replaces the ordinary random walk iteration typically performed in label spreading. Due to the multilevel nature of algebraic multigrid solvers, AMELS spreads given label information across a graph of any size in a single multigrid cycle. We demonstrate that AMELS achieves significant runtime reductions compared to existing implementations while also being more robust to hyperparameter choices in terms of both runtime and classification accuracy. Our framework therefore enables efficient label spreading on large-scale image datasets and produces accurate labels even when only a few labeled samples are available.

</details>


### [249] [Privacy Without Regret: Differentially Private Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26324)
*Ishi Jain,Nandini Bhattad,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过向奖励分数添加校准的Gumbel噪声，同时解决Best-of-N采样中的奖励黑客问题和敏感人类偏好数据的隐私保护问题。作者建立了Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN)方法，证明在隐私预算超过临界阈值时，隐私强制噪声即为最优正则化，且隐私不产生额外对齐成本。进一步提出Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP)，结合χ²正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制，实现与响应数量无关的隐私成本，并在多个模型和数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N (BoN)采样是最广泛使用的推理时对齐策略，但存在两个独立问题：一是奖励黑客现象，即选中的响应利用代理奖励模型的缺陷；二是训练奖励模型所需的敏感人类偏好数据缺乏隐私保护。现有方法分别处理这两个问题，本文旨在证明单一干预措施可同时解决二者。

Method: 本文提出两种方法。第一，PrivBoN：在选择前向奖励分数添加适当尺度的Gumbel噪声，同时实现ε-差分隐私和KL正则化对齐；证明隐私预算超过临界阈值ε*时，隐私强制噪声即为遗憾最优的正则化。第二，PrivITP：结合χ²正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制，达到ex-post (ε,δ)-DP，隐私成本与响应数量n无关，并实现正则化参数与隐私参数的解耦。

Result: 实验覆盖多个语言模型、数据集和奖励模型，结果显示：PrivBoN和PrivITP均具有缩放单调性（而BoN在超过临界n后性能退化）；在相同隐私水平下PrivITP匹配或优于PrivBoN，且在强隐私机制下增益最大。

Conclusion: 单一干预——向奖励分数添加校准噪声——可同时解决BoN的两个核心问题（奖励黑客和隐私缺失）。PrivBoN建立了隐私与正则化对齐之间的理论等价性，PrivITP进一步放松了对未知覆盖率系数的依赖，实现了更实用的强隐私保护方案，且二者均优于传统BoN的缩放行为。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is the simplest and most widely deployed inference-time alignment strategy, but it suffers from two distinct problems: reward hacking, in which the selected response exploits errors in the proxy reward model, and the absence of any privacy protection for the sensitive human preference data used to train that reward model. We show that a single intervention-adding calibrated noise to reward scores before selection-resolves both. Our first result, Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN), establishes that Gumbel noise at an appropriate scale simultaneously provides $ε$-differential privacy and implements KL-regularized alignment. Whenever the privacy budget exceeds a critical threshold $ε^*$, the privacy-mandated noise is the regret-optimal regularization, and privacy imposes zero additional alignment cost-matching the information-theoretic skyline of Huang et al. (2025). Because $ε^*$ depends on an unknown coverage coefficient, we introduce Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP), which combines $χ^2$-regularized rejection sampling with a two-phase Gaussian mechanism. PrivITP achieves ex-post $(ε,δ)$-DP with a privacy cost independent of the number of responses $n$, cleanly decouples the regularization parameter from the privacy parameter, and attains the skyline up to a noise-inflation term. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm our results: PrivBoN and PrivITP are scaling-monotonic (unlike BoN, which degrades past a critical $n$), and PrivITP matches or outperforms PrivBoN at equivalent privacy levels, with the largest gains in the strong-privacy regime.

</details>


### [250] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为"隐式诊断分类法"(Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy)的框架，用于构建分类器作为防护层，并开发互补的诊断方法来识别分类器自信决策中哪些可以被信任。该框架通过维度优化、潜在支持向量定位和诊断分类三个步骤，将提示词划分为安全依赖、启发式偏置/覆盖、以及需要人工审查的不足上下文三类。实验发现约77%的自信决策在移除单个标记后不再稳健，暴露出置信度校准失败和可被利用的捷径两种故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对分类器输出的信任缺乏系统性评估手段，尤其是当分类器给出"自信"决策时，用户往往盲目依赖。需要一种端到端框架来诊断哪些自信决策真正稳健、哪些存在脆弱性，从而为不同的提示词分配不同的处理策略（安全依赖/标记风险/人工审查）。

Method: 框架包含三个核心步骤：(i) 构建维度优化的分类器——通过交叉验证性能而非先验固定来经验性选择嵌入维度；(ii) 定位约29%训练样本的潜在支持向量，代表对识别可改变预测标签的令牌最具影响力的提示；(iii) 利用这些令牌及其关联的攻击强度构建诊断分类法，将提示词划分为：安全依赖(Safely)、启发式偏置(Heuristic Bias)、启发式覆盖(Heuristic Override)和不足上下文(Insufficient Context)四个区域，并针对各区域推荐修复策略。

Result: 在公开的提示注入数据集上，发现约77%的自信决策在移除单个令牌后不再稳健，该脆弱性可区分为两种不同的失败模式：置信度校准失败与真正可利用的捷径。分类法成功将置信度失败与可被利用的漏洞区分开来。

Conclusion: 该框架为分类器信任决策提供了端到端的诊断指南：对于可靠决策可直接依赖；启发式偏置和覆盖案例需标记；上下文不足案例需转交人工/安全审查。同时针对每个分类区域推荐了相应的修复策略，并以系列步骤演示了框架的运作方式。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [251] [Beyond Capability Benchmarks: Learning Operational Fingerprints of LLM Cloud Services from Production Incident Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26332)
*Meiwei Zhang,Eduardo Miranda,Bruce Baynes,Suvigya Jain,Wanlong Chen,Tao He,Sergey Borodavkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpEmbed是一个用于从隐私保护的运维工单元数据中学习LLM云服务紧凑运维指纹（operational fingerprints）的框架，在Google Cloud超过33,000条生产工单、26个月、7个LLM家族的评估中，能够恢复可解释的家族和版本级结构，并改善留一模型外的运维预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管LLM服务已进入真实生产系统，但模型选择和服务的规划仍严重依赖能力基准（capability benchmarks），而这些基准对部署后的运维行为揭示甚少。现有方法缺乏对LLM云服务运维特性的表征与预测手段。

Method: OpEmbed将模型-时间窗口聚合为八通道运维签名（operational signature），通过时间对比学习（temporal contrastive learning）、跨视图重建（cross-view reconstruction）和代际序数正则化（generational-ordinality regularization）学习低维表示。框架基于结构化的、隐私保护的工单元数据，不使用工单文本内容。

Result: 在Google Cloud超过33,000条生产工单（覆盖7个LLM家族、26个月）上进行评估：OpEmbed能恢复可解释的家族级和版本级结构；在留一模型外（leave-one-model-out）运维预测上优于非学习基线；在早期窗口数据有限的情况下仍保持有用性；并支持跨模型的故障类型迁移。

Conclusion: OpEmbed为LLM云服务的模型选型、运维就绪评估（support readiness assessment）和运维监控提供了实用的运维表征工具，并总结了生产环境中构建和评估该工具的实际经验教训。

Abstract: Managed LLM services are now part of real production systems, but model selection and service planning still rely heavily on capability benchmarks that reveal little about operational behavior after deployment. We present Operational Embedding (OpEmbed), a framework for learning compact operational fingerprints of LLM cloud services from structured, privacy-preserving support-case metadata, without using case text. OpEmbed aggregates model--time windows into an eight-channel operational signature and learns a low-dimensional representation via temporal contrastive learning, cross-view reconstruction, and generational-ordinality regularization. Evaluated on more than 33,000 production support cases spanning seven LLM families over 26 months at Google Cloud, OpEmbed recovers interpretable family- and version-level structure, improves leave-one-model-out operational forecasting over non-learned baselines, remains useful under limited early-window data, and supports cross-model fault-type transfer. We report the practical lessons learned from building and evaluating this tool for model onboarding, support readiness assessment, and operational monitoring.

</details>


### [252] [CG4AI: A Column Generation Framework for Training AI Models Under Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26375)
*Youcef Magnouche,Abderrahmane Driouch,Sébastien Martin,Pierre Bauguion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CG4AI框架，通过主线性规划确定AI模型的凸组合权重，结合定价子问题（由LP对偶变量引导）和切割平面过程，在保证模型输出满足线性约束的前提下提升预测精度。在MNIST数字分类和多商品流路由两个问题上验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习训练只最小化损失函数，不保证模型输出满足预定义规则或约束。在自动驾驶、网络路由等真实应用中，这种约束保证至关重要，因此需要一种能在训练过程中显式纳入约束条件的框架。

Method: CG4AI框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 一个主线性规划（LP），用于确定多个AI模型的凸组合权重，同时强制线性约束；(2) 一个定价子问题，利用LP对偶变量生成新模型，聚焦于最违反约束的区域；(3) 一个切割平面过程，将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。

Result: 在MNIST上展示了四种约束用法（仅从约束学习、提升对抗鲁棒性、修正误分类样本、强制输出重标记）；在多商品流问题中强制链路容量约束。实验表明CG4AI能可靠地产生可行预测器，且在SNDLIB基准网络上的准确率优于单模型基线。

Conclusion: CG4AI通过列生成（column generation）思想将约束满足与模型组合有机结合，在保证输出可行性的同时提升了预测性能，为需要严格约束保证的AI应用提供了一种系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Standard machine-learning training minimizes a loss function over a dataset, but does not guarantee that the resulting model will satisfy predefined rules or constraints on its outputs. In many real-world applications, ranging from autonomous systems to network routing, such guarantees are essential. We propose CG4AI, a framework that builds a convex combination of AI models while enforcing linear constraints on the combined output. A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [253] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCMAPSS，一个基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集的标准联邦RUL估计基准，定义了五个模拟真实工业场景的标准任务（从理想IID到极端统计异构），并系统评估了多种联邦优化算法与神经架构，提供了可复现的基线与公开代码和数据划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然联邦学习为无需共享传感器数据即可协作训练RUL预测模型提供了有前景的范式，但现有研究缺乏统一的评估框架，使得不同方法之间难以进行公平比较。

Method: 基于C-MAPSS数据集构建联邦基准FedCMAPSS，定义了五个标准化任务，覆盖从理想IID设置到极端统计异质性等真实工业场景，并跨多种神经架构系统评估了最先进的联邦优化算法。

Result: 建立了可复现的基线结果，公开发布了源代码和数据划分方案。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在为开发和比较联邦预测性维护解决方案提供标准化的基础平台。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset. We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [254] [Beyond Client Averaging: A Client-Independent Second-Order Stationary-Bias Component in Stochastic SCAFFOLD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26765)
*Yi-Ping Tang,Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在完全参与、一维同质客户端、固定本地步数H和有界加性梯度噪声设定下，证明随机SCAFFOLD的稳态均值偏差在系数层面存在与客户端数N无关的O(γ²)项，并给出了精确系数表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有常量步长分析仅能识别随机SCAFFOLD的O(γ/N)主导稳态均值偏差，并指出高阶偏差可随客户端数增加而持续存在，但未在系数层面识别首个与客户端数无关的贡献项。

Method: 在一维同质客户端、固定本地步数H、完全参与和有界加性梯度噪声条件下，通过统一地对N≥2进行证明，推导出稳态均值偏差在γ/N和γ²两个量级上的精确系数表达式，并分析其余项。

Result: 证明稳态均值偏差等于 -f'''(x*)σ²γ/(4f''(x*)²N) - f'''(x*)σ²(H-1)(5H-1)γ²/(12f''(x*)H) + O_H(γ²/N + γ³)。客户端平均抑制了主导的O(γ/N)偏差，但无法消除系数非零时与客户端数无关的O(γ²)分量。

Conclusion: 该偏差产生机制是间接的：虽然直接控制贡献在全局线性平均中逐路径抵消，但控制仍会改变轮内局部轨迹及其二阶矩，新鲜梯度噪声与持续控制波动产生局部二阶矩修正，非二次曲率将其转化为稳态均值偏差。系数在二次目标下为零。数值实验与预测系数、随客户端数增加的持续性及联合余项一致。结果限于一维同质固定-H设定。

Abstract: Existing constant-step analysis of stochastic \Scaf{} identifies a leading $O(γ/N)$ stationary mean bias and shows that higher-order bias can persist as the client count increases, but does not identify the first client-independent contribution at coefficient level. For full-participation stochastic \Scaf{} with one-dimensional homogeneous clients, fixed local-step count $H$, and bounded additive gradient noise, we prove, uniformly over $N\ge2$,
  $$ \begin{aligned} \mathbb{E}_{π_{γ,N,H}}[x]-x^\star ={}& -\frac{f'''(x^\star)σ^2}{4f''(x^\star)^2}\fracγ{N}\\ &- \frac{f'''(x^\star)σ^2}{12f''(x^\star)} \frac{(H-1)(5H-1)}{H}γ^2 +O_H\!\left(\frac{γ^2}{N}+γ^3\right). \end{aligned} $$
  Hence client averaging suppresses the leading $O(γ/N)$ bias but does not remove the client-independent $O(γ^2)$ component when its coefficient is nonzero.
  The mechanism is indirect: although the direct control contribution cancels pathwise in the linear global average, the controls still alter within-round local trajectories and their second moments. Fresh gradient noise and persistent control fluctuations therefore generate local second-moment corrections that nonquadratic curvature converts into stationary mean bias. The coefficient vanishes for quadratic objectives. Numerical experiments are consistent with the predicted coefficient, its persistence as client count increases, and the stated joint remainder. The result is restricted to the one-dimensional homogeneous fixed-$H$ setting.

</details>


### [255] [NeoTriFuse: Reliability-Aware Multimodal Fusion under Missingness Heterogeneity for Neonatal Mortality Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26436)
*Jiyuan Tian,Qincheng Shen,Ye Lin,Yu Gao,Haohui Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了NeoTriFuse，一个可靠性感知的多模态融合框架，用于新生儿死亡率预测。该框架将数据缺失视为可靠性信号而非预处理问题，通过可靠性引导的门控机制动态调节各模态贡献，联合优化死亡率预测和住院时长辅助目标，在F1=0.6736、AUROC=0.9454上取得competitive性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿死亡率预测面临极端类别不平衡、临床风险因素异质性、多尺度时间动态性和大量数据缺失等四大挑战。现有多模态方法将缺失性视为预处理问题，未能充分利用缺失模式中蕴含的临床信息，因此需要一种将缺失性显式建模为可靠性信号、动态调节模态贡献的融合框架。

Method: NeoTriFuse包含三个核心部分：(1) 静态围产期变量、局部-全局时间编码器、患者级统计摘要三种模态；(2) 可靠性引导的门控机制，根据数据缺失模式动态调节各模态在融合中的贡献权重；(3) 联合优化目标，同时训练死亡率预测主任务和住院时长辅助任务。整体架构将缺失性显式建模为可靠性信号，而非简单预处理。

Result: NeoTriFuse在F1分数上达到0.6736±0.0216，AUROC达到0.9454±0.0056。消融实验显示局部-全局时间架构和患者级统计摘要分支对性能贡献最大，可靠性感知门控在异质观测完整性条件下对阈值依赖指标提供额外改进。敏感性分析表明在邻近超参数设置下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的多模态融合是在现实临床缺失条件下进行新生儿死亡率预测的实用方法，证实了将缺失性作为可靠性信号进行显式建模的有效性，而非将其仅视为需要预处理的数据质量问题。

Abstract: Neonatal mortality risk prediction from bedside monitoring data remains challenging due to extreme class imbalance, heterogeneous clinical risk factors, multi-scale temporal dynamics, and substantial missingness. We propose NeoTriFuse, a reliability-aware multimodal fusion framework for missingness-heterogeneous neonatal monitoring data. Unlike conventional multimodal approaches that treat missingness primarily as a preprocessing issue, NeoTriFuse models missingness as an explicit reliability signal that dynamically modulates modality contributions during fusion. The framework integrates static perinatal variables, local-global temporal encoders, and patient-level statistical summaries through reliability-guided gating mechanisms, while jointly optimizing mortality prediction and an auxiliary length-of-stay objective. NeoTriFuse achieves competitive performance, with an F1 score of 0.6736 +/- 0.0216 and an AUROC of 0.9454 +/- 0.0056. Ablation studies indicate that the local-global temporal architecture and patient-level summary branch contribute most substantially to predictive performance, while reliability-aware gating provides additional improvements on threshold-dependent metrics under heterogeneous observation completeness. Sensitivity analyses further suggest stable performance across nearby hyperparameter settings. Overall, the findings support reliability-aware multimodal fusion as a practical approach for neonatal mortality prediction under realistic clinical missingness conditions.

</details>


### [256] [Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26437)
*Haohui Lu,jiyuan Tian,Fangyu Zhou,Shahadat Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SF-GNN，一种轻量且架构无关的框架，通过识别并随机过滤有偏见的边缘来从源头缓解图神经网络中的结构性偏见，在保持预测性能的同时显著提升公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平感知GNN方法主要在全局层面约束表示或预测分布，未能显式控制聚合过程中偏见信息传播的局部结构路径，因此需要从源头（结构路径）控制偏见传播的方法。

Method: SF-GNN通过结合敏感同质性与结构传播放大器（包括中枢节点参与和三角闭合）识别易产生偏见的边缘，然后在每次消息传递中引入随机边缘过滤以选择性降权或移除这些边缘，同时保留其余图结构；训练中引入带预热机制的统计均等正则化器以稳定优化。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，SF-GNN实现了持续的公平性提升，同时保持有竞争力的预测性能，在公平性-准确性权衡上优于近期的公平感知GNN基线方法。

Conclusion: 从结构层面阻断偏见传播通路比全局约束更有效，且轻量化的架构无关设计使SF-GNN易于集成到现有GNN中，实现了公平与准确性的更优平衡。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can exhibit unfair behavior even when sensitive attributes are excluded from node features, because graph topology and message passing propagate group-correlated signals under sensitive homophily. Existing fairness-aware GNN methods mainly constrain representations or prediction distributions at a global level, without explicitly controlling the local structural pathways through which biased information propagates during aggregation. We propose Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks (SF-GNN), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that mitigates structural bias at its source. SF-GNN identifies bias-prone edges by combining sensitive homophily with structural propagation amplifiers, including hub participation and triadic closure. It then incorporates stochastic edge filtering into each message-passing step to selectively downweight or remove these edges while preserving the remaining graph structure. Training further incorporates a statistical-parity regularizer with a warm-up schedule to stabilize optimization. Experiments on five benchmark datasets show that SF-GNN achieves consistent fairness improvements while maintaining competitive predictive performance, leading to a better fairness--accuracy trade-off than recent fairness-aware GNN baselines.

</details>


### [257] [Toward Equitable Low-Carbon Mobility: Fairness-Aware Demand Prediction for Expanding Bike-Sharing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26451)
*Man Luo,Yixuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairGIN是一个面向扩展中共享单车系统的公平感知图神经网络，通过扩展模拟增量训练、注意力知识迁移和公平感知优化三个组件，同时解决冷启动预测和收入不均衡的资源分配问题，在纽约和西雅图数据集上实现了最先进的预测精度并显著降低了收入差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车系统的持续扩展带来两大挑战：新站点缺乏历史骑行记录导致图模型的冷启动预测分布不匹配；历史需求数据可能编码结构性不平等，低收入社区的低骑行量可能反映基础设施获取受限而非潜在需求不足，直接训练会强化现有出行不平等。

Method: FairGIN由三个组件构成：(1) 扩展模拟增量训练，在训练期间随机模拟网络扩展以缩小冷启动分布差距；(2) 基于注意力的知识迁移，结合站点自适应温度缩放与正交嵌入对齐，将数据丰富的现有站点的表示迁移到数据稀少的新闻站；(3) 公平感知优化，引入收入分层正则化和公平校准部署评分，支持更包容的站点布局。

Result: 在纽约和西雅图数据集上的实验表明，FairGIN在各种扩展场景中取得了最先进的预测精度，同时在不牺牲整体系统效率的前提下大幅降低了基于收入的差异。

Conclusion: FairGIN验证了在共享单车需求预测中同时兼顾预测精度和公平性是可行的，表明通过专门设计的图神经网络架构可以有效缓解冷启动问题并减少收入层面的出行不平等。

Abstract: Bike-sharing systems are an important component of low-carbon urban mobility, but continued expansion creates challenges in both cold-start prediction and equitable resource allocation. Newly deployed stations lack historical ridership records, causing a mismatch between training and inference for graph-based models on evolving networks. Historical demand may also encode structural inequalities, as lower ridership in low-income neighborhoods can reflect limited infrastructure access rather than weak latent demand. Models trained directly on such data may therefore reinforce existing mobility disparities. We propose FairGIN, a fairness-aware graph neural network for demand prediction in expanding bike-sharing systems. FairGIN integrates three components. Expansion-Simulated Increment Training stochastically simulates network expansion during training to reduce the cold-start distribution gap. Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer combines station-adaptive temperature scaling with orthogonal embedding alignment to transfer representations from data-rich existing stations to data-sparse new stations. Fairness-Aware Optimization introduces income-stratified regularization and an equity-calibrated deployment score to support more inclusive station placement. Experiments on NYC and Seattle demonstrate that FairGIN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy across diverse expansion scenarios while substantially reducing income-based disparities without compromising overall system efficiency.

</details>


### [258] [Distributed Training using an Intelligent Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26453)
*Nihar Shah,Ben Blier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将广域网（WAN）网络本身作为分布式训练的主动参与者：在系统层面利用组播技术和在线FPGA分别缓解出站和入站流量瓶颈；在算法层面开发优化框架，围绕网络拓扑生成丰富的同步调度方案（轮转的岛屿团），从而缩小跨WAN分布式训练与同地训练之间的差距。作者在基于DoubleZero网络的九城市拓扑上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨广域网（WAN）的分布式训练极具挑战性，因为计算岛屿之间持续的参数交换受限于有限带宽、高延迟和不均衡的网络拓扑，严重制约了训练效率。

Method: 系统层面：(i) 利用组播技术复制出站流量以缓解出口瓶颈；(ii) 利用在线FPGA聚合入站流量以缓解入口瓶颈，并将这些原本仅用于数据中心内部的技术扩展到WAN。算法层面：开发优化框架，围绕网络拓扑生成丰富的同步调度方案，即轮转的岛屿团（rotating cliques of islands），以最大化信息交换。最后在基于DoubleZero网络的九城市拓扑上进行验证，展示最优调度随网络能力的变化。

Result: 在仿照DoubleZero网络建模的九城市拓扑上，实验表明所提出的优化调度方案能够随着网络能力的变化自适应调整最优调度策略，从而显著缩小与同地训练（colocated training）这一黄金标准之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 将网络作为分布式训练的主动参与者（而非仅视为被动传输通道），结合组播与FPGA等网络技术以及围绕拓扑的优化同步调度，可以有效缓解WAN带宽和延迟约束对分布式训练的制约，弥合与本地集群训练的性能鸿沟。

Abstract: Distributed training across a wide area network (WAN) is challenging, as continuous parameter exchange by islands of compute is constrained by limited bandwidth, high latency, and uneven topology. We propose making the network an active participant in training. On the systems side, such networks should leverage (i) multicast technology to replicate outbound traffic and (ii) in-line FPGAs to aggregate inbound traffic, to ease egress and ingress bottlenecks. These technologies are used for training across workers within a data center, but this paper extends them to the WAN. On the algorithms side, we develop an optimization framework that produces rich synchronization schedules (namely, rotating cliques of islands) around the underlying network topology and these technologies, to maximize information exchange. Finally, we illustrate this on a nine-city topology modeled on the DoubleZero network, a live programmable WAN equipped with both technologies, and show how the optimal schedules shift with the network's capabilities. Together, these can narrow the gap to the gold standard of colocated training.

</details>


### [259] [Diff Mining: Logit Differences Reveal Finetuning Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26462)
*Greg Kocher,Robert West,Clément Dumas,Julian Minder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff Mining框架，通过比较微调模型与基础模型的输出logits来识别微调过程中模型学习到的行为模式。该方法无需访问模型内部结构，仅依赖输出logits即可在大规模模型上运行，并在微调领域检测和偏见识别等任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已成为语言模型改进行为和引入新能力的标准方法，但微调过程中究竟产生了哪些行为仍不明确。随着模型能力增强，理解微调过程变得愈发重要，尤其是微调可能引入不期望的行为。现有模型diffing方法多需要模型内部信息，可扩展性受限。

Method: Diff Mining包含两个模块化阶段：(i)在参考语料上逐上下文提取微调模型与基础模型之间的logit差异；(ii)聚合差异信号以构建可解释的token集合。聚合方法探索了简单的Top-K频率方法和基于非负矩阵分解（NMF）的方法，后者可将多个微调目标解耦为不同的token簇。

Result: 在微调领域检测任务上，Diff Mining在识别相关token和下游性能方面显著优于最先进的模型diffing方法；在注入偏见的模型中，无需定向探测即可识别超过三分之一的偏见；框架在多种设置下均表现成功。

Conclusion: Diff Mining展示了无需模型内部访问即可有效识别微调目标的前景，为开发微调目标审计工具提供了有希望的路径，对模型安全和可解释性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Finetuning has become the gold standard for refining existing behaviors and inducing new ones in language models, yet it often remains unclear exactly which behaviors emerge during this process. As models grow ever more capable, understanding finetuning better becomes increasingly important, particularly since unwanted behaviors may arise during finetuning. In this paper, we introduce Diff Mining, a simple yet effective framework for identifying what a finetuned model has learned by comparing its logits to those of its base model. Diff Mining effectively surfaces salient tokens that are amplified in the finetuned model, serving as a fingerprint of its training -- even on text unrelated to the finetuning domain. Unlike many existing model diffing methods which require model internals, Diff Mining only needs access to output logits and scales to large models. The framework consists of two modular stages: (i) extracting per-context logit differences between the finetuned and base models on a reference corpus, and (ii) aggregating the resulting signals to construct an interpretable token set representing the finetune. For aggregation, we explore both a simple Top-K frequency method and a Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF)-based approach for disentangling multiple finetuning objectives into distinct token clusters. Empirically, Diff Mining succeeds across diverse settings: on finetune domain detection, it significantly outperforms state-of-the-art model diffing methods both in identifying relevant tokens and in downstream performance when an interpretability agent is given access to the extracted token set; on models with injected biases, it identifies more than one third of the biases without targeted probing. Overall, our framework shows promise in developing auditing tools to detect finetuning objectives.

</details>


### [260] [Active Curriculum Refinement for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26469)
*Zhenya Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATH是一个课程学习框架，通过显式建模强化学习环境之间由前提关系（如难度递增编辑或参数增量）诱导的有向无环课程图（DAG），在该图上执行主动学习。它先采样多样化的课程路径以扩大覆盖度，再重新分配训练资源到未掌握区域，从而在多种环境中实现强大的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多RL环境中环境之间由前提关系（如难度递进的编辑或参数增量）相互连接，形成有向无环课程图（DAG）。这种结构通常仅被隐式利用，作者认为显式建模该结构可以提升训练效果。

Method: 提出PATH框架，一种在课程图上进行主动学习的课程学习方法。该方法包含两个核心步骤：(1)扩展覆盖度——采样多样化的课程路径以探索图中不同区域；(2)重新分配——将训练资源重新分配到尚未掌握的图区域。

Result: 在多种不同环境中进行的实验表明，PATH通过显式利用课程图结构，实现了强大的鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模并主动利用课程图结构可以显著提升RL课程学习的训练效果，PATH框架通过路径采样与资源重分配策略在多种环境下验证了该思路的有效性。

Abstract: In many reinforcement learning (RL) domains, environments are connected by prerequisite relations, such as difficulty-increasing edits or parameter increments, which induce a directed acyclic curriculum graph (DAG). Although this structure is often exploited only implicitly, explicitly modeling it can improve training. We introduce PATH, a curriculum-learning framework that performs active learning over the curriculum graph. PATH first expands coverage by sampling diverse curriculum paths and then reallocates training toward regions that remain unmastered. Experiments across diverse environments show that PATH explicitly leverages the graph structure to achieve strong robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [261] [Shared Actors Need Not Share Critics: Effects of Value Mismatch in Parallel Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26481)
*Zhenya Liu,Yang Meng,Zhuokai Zhao,Xuefeng Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了并行强化学习中共享Critic导致的价值不匹配问题：不同环境对相同输入分配不同期望回报，Critic被迫调和这些不同目标，系统性偏移优势估计。作者通过bandit模型刻画了这一机制，提出仅给Critic条件化一个环境索引的极简干预（multihead conditional critic），在Procgen全部16个游戏、600个未见关卡上平均归一化回报提升40.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在并行强化学习中（如程序生成关卡、随机动力学或课程学习），常见实现在所有采样环境间共享一个Critic。但不同环境可能对Critic可见的相同输入赋予不同期望回报，导致Critic必须调和冲突的价值目标，从而系统性偏移各个环境内的优势估计。这一价值不匹配的机制尚未被理论化刻画，且其影响无法仅用标量估计器方差来捕捉。作者旨在识别这一直接损伤随机学习动力学（而非仅估计精度）的机制，并寻求最小干预方案。

Method: 理论部分采用带多个环境及公共最优臂的可解释bandit模型，刻画Critic共享造成的价值不匹配如何在固定策略下重新分配采样的策略更新——强化无用动作、削弱甚至反转有用动作。作者对比了无基线（oracle无基线）、共享值基线和环境特定值基线三种过程，证明它们在固定策略下具有相同的平均logit更新并收敛到同一最优策略，但学习路径可显著不同。实验部分提出极简干预：仅向Critic传入一个记录的环境索引，使其能够分离价值目标（multihead conditional critic）。在受控的CartPole和MuJoCo环境中验证预测的偏移值、优势与性能差异，并在更复杂的BipedalWalker和Procgen设置中检验稳定性和回报。

Result: 理论证明：使用共享值基线与环境特定值基线在固定策略下具有相同平均logit更新并收敛到同一最优策略，但实际学习路径差异显著。受控的CartPole和MuJoCo实验证实了预测的价值偏移、优势偏移和性能差距。BipedalWalker和Procgen中同一干预带来更稳定的学习与更高回报。在全部16个Procgen游戏中，multihead conditional critic在每游戏600个未见关卡上使聚合归一化回报提升40.8%。

Conclusion: 价值不匹配是Critic共享损伤随机学习动力学的直接机制，其影响无法由标量估计器方差单独捕捉。对Critic条件化环境索引这一极简干预在并行强化学习中广泛有效，为实际部署提供了低成本且可解释的改进方案。
